window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const compiled=RenderPart4Core.compile(job);
    const backgroundFallback=RenderColor.hexToLinear(job.settings.background||'#202020');
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const primaryBatch16=new RenderRayBatch16(Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)));
    const rowChunk=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));
    await RenderTileScheduler16.runLocal(job,tiles16,async tile=>{
      for(let y=tile.y;y<tile.y+tile.height;y++){
        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreC=RenderCoreC;

  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  });

  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMateria

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,light.intensity);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=1/(dist*dist);
    if(light.range>0){
      const f=Math.max(0,1-dist/light.range);
      attenuation*=f*f;
    }

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:RenderColor.mul(light.color,light.intensity*attenuation*emitterCos/safeArea),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMateria

        if(d<this.radius&&d<bestD&&RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal)>=LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold){best=id;bestD=d;}

  stats(){return Object.freeze({records:this.records.length,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,inserts:this.inserts,rejected:this.rejected,retraces:this.retraces,refreshes:this.refreshes,radius:this.radius});}
}

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];
    let throughput=[1,1,1];
    let ray=primaryRay;
    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){
      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

      if(!hit.hit){
        const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,ray.direction));
        radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,env));
        if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
          const w=Math.max(0,.2126*env[0]+.7152*env[1]+.0722*env[2]);
          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;
      }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      //
--- occurrence 8 at 372180 ---
apped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

var PathGIRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;
    job.lightTree=RenderCoreH.createLightTree(job,compiled);
    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;
    const accumulator=new ProgressiveAccumulator(width,height);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const primaryMask=new Uint8Array(width*height);
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;
    const maxSamples=Math.max(1,settings.maxSamples||1);
    const progressive=settings.progressive!==false;
    const passLimit=progressive?maxSamples:1;
    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));

      let sampledThisPass=0;
      const activePixels16=progressive?accumulator.compactActive(settings):(()=>{
        const a=new Uint32Array(width*height);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=i;return a;
      })();
      if(activePixels16.length===0)break;

      const workQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16.fromIndices(activePixels16,Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*4));
      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;
          const jitterX=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,20)-.5;
          const jitterY=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,21)-.5;
          const ray=RenderPart5Core.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x+jitterX,rr.y0+y+jitterY,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){
            const ph=sample.primaryHitRecord;
            if(ph?.hit)RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,ph,seed));
            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();
      onProgress(Math.min(1,completedPasses/passLimit));

      if(!progressive)break;
      if(settings.adaptive&&completedPasses>=settings.minSamples&&accumulator.active===0)break;
      if(sampledThisPass===0)break;
    }

    const rgba=acc

  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }

    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  }
}

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot(){
  return Object.freeze({
    lifecycle:ThreeDLiteAppContext.lifecycle,
    phase:ThreeDLiteAppContext.phase,
    services:Object.freeze(Object.keys(ThreeDLiteAppContext.services)),
    failures:Object.freeze(ThreeDLiteAppContext.failures.slice()),
    dependencyCheck:validate3DLiteServiceDependencies()
  });
}
window.get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot=get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot;

function validate3DLiteServiceDependencies(){
  const missing=[];
  for(const [name,deps] of Object.entries(ThreeDLiteSubsystemRegistry.dependencies)){
    if(!ThreeDLiteAppContext.get(name))continue;
    for(const dep of deps)if(!ThreeDLiteAppContext.get(dep))missing.push(`${name}->${dep}`);
  }
  return Object.freeze({ok:missing.length===0,missing});
}
window.validate3DLiteServiceDependencies=validate3DLiteServiceDependencies;

function freezeRenderSnapshot(v){
  if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||Object.isFrozen(v)||ArrayBuffer.isView(v))return v;
  Object.freeze(v);for(const k of Object.keys(v))freezeRenderSnapshot(v[k]);return v;
}

  constructor(d){

function get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot(){
  return Object.freeze({
    lifecycle:ThreeDLiteAppContext.lifecycle,
    phase:ThreeDLiteAppContext.phase,
    services:Object.freeze(Object.keys(ThreeDLiteAppContext.services)),
    failures:Object.freeze(ThreeDLiteAppContext.failures.slice()),
    dependencyCheck:validate3DLiteServiceDependencies()
  });
}
window.get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot=get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot;

function validate3DLiteServiceDependencies(){
  const missing=[];
  for(const [name,deps] of Object.entries(ThreeDLiteSubsystemRegistry.dependencies)){
    if(!ThreeDLiteAppContext.get(name))continue;
    for(const dep of deps)if(!ThreeDLiteAppContext.get(dep))missing.push(`${name}->${dep}`);
  }
  return Object.freeze({ok:missing.length===0,missing});
}
window.validate3DLiteServiceDependencies=validate3DLiteServiceDependencies;

function freezeRenderSnapshot(v){
  if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||Object.isFrozen(v)||ArrayBuffer.isView(v))return v;
  Object.freeze(v);for(const k of Object.keys(v))freezeRenderSnapshot(v[k]);return v;
}

    this.bounds=freezeRenderSnapshot(d.bounds);Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const compiled=RenderPart4Core.compile(job);
    const backgroundFallback=RenderColor.hexToLinear(job.settings.background||'#202020');
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const primaryBatch16=new RenderRayBatch16(Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)));
    const rowChunk=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));
    await RenderTileScheduler16.runLocal(job,tiles16,async tile=>{
      for(let y=tile.y;y<tile.y+tile.height;y++){
        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreC=RenderCoreC;

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreC=RenderCoreC;

  consumes:Object.freeze([

    'Core A render settings'

    'editor viewport materials',
    'full scene light object system',
    'shadow visibility',
    'area-light sampling',
    'path GI',
    'light cache',
    'denoising'
  ])
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,light.intensity);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=1/(dist*dist);
    if(light.range>0){
      const f=Math.max(0,1-dist/light.range);
      attenuation*=f*f;
    }

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:RenderColor.mul(light.color,light.intensity*attenuation*emitterCos/safeArea),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];
    let throughput=[1,1,1];
    let ray=primaryRay;
    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){
      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

      if(!hit.hit){
        const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,ray.direction));
        radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,env));
        if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
          const w=Math.max(0,.2126*env[0]+.7152*env[1]+.0722*env[2]);
          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;
      }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environm
--- occurrence 12 at 372189 ---

      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

const ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext=Object.freeze({
  create(job){
    if(!job)throw new Error('RenderExecutionContext requires a render job');
    return Object.seal({
      job,
      scheduler:ThreeDLiteRenderSchedulerService.implementation(),
      workers:ThreeDLiteWorkerService.implementation(),
      acceleration:ThreeDLiteAccelerationService.implementation(),
      performanceState:ThreeDLitePerformanceService.attach(job),
      createdAt:globalThis.performance?.now?.()??Date.now()
    });
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext=ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext;

const ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionServices=Object.freeze({
  get scheduler(){return ThreeDLiteRenderSchedulerService},
  get workers(){return ThreeDLiteWorkerService},
  get acceleration(){return ThreeDLiteAccelerationService},
  get sampling(){return ThreeDLiteSamplingService},
  get performance(){return ThreeDLitePerformanceService},
  get context(){return ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext}
});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionServices=ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionServices;

function get3DLiteRenderExecutionSnapshot(job=null){
  const j=job||window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;
  return Object.freeze({
    jobId:j?.id??null,
    workerAvailable:ThreeDLiteWorkerService.available(),
    schedulerAvailable:!!ThreeDLiteRenderSchedulerService.implementation(),
    accelerationAvailable:!!ThreeDLiteAccelerationService.implementation(),
    samplingAvailable:!!ThreeDLiteSamplingService.implementation(),
    performanceAvailable:!!ThreeDLitePerformanceService.implementation(),
    hasPerformanceState:!!ThreeDLitePerformanceService.metrics(j)
  });
}

function validateArchitectureCore(){
  const errors=[];
  const required={SelectionManager,InputController,ToolManager,CommandSystem,HistoryManager,MeshValidator,MeshOps,DerivedMeshCache,Evaluator,ViewportDisplay,ThreeViewportBackend,PickingSystem,MaterialManager,UVWSystem,ParameterSystem,TransformSystem,TransformHitPolicy,PivotManager,SnapManager,DragSolver,SceneModel};
  for(const [name,value] of Object.entries(required))if(!value)errors.push(`missing ${name}`);
  if(typeof syncTransformTarget!=='function')errors.push('missing syncTransformTarget');
  if(typeof updateDisplayForMesh!=='function')errors.push('missing updateDisplayForMesh');
  if(typeof MeshWireManager==='undefined')errors.push('missing MeshWireManager');
  if(typeof OverlayManager==='undefined')errors.push('missing OverlayManager');
  if(typeof MNMesh!=='function'||typeof MNEdge!=='function'||typeof MNFace!=='function')errors.push('missing MNMesh kernel');
  if(typeof DerivedObject!=='function'||typeof SceneNode!=='function')errors.push('missing object pipeline');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const ThreeDLiteVersion=Object.freeze({name:'3DLite',version:'3.97.7'});
const LiteTraceVersion=Object.freeze({name:'LiteTrace Renderer',version:'1.1 RC1'});
window.ThreeDLiteVersion=ThreeDLiteVersion;
window.LiteTraceVersion=LiteTraceVersion;

const ThreeDLiteBootPhase=Object.freeze({
  DECLARATION:'declaration',
  REGISTRATION:'registration',
  EDITOR:'editor',
  VIEWPORT:'viewport',
  RENDERER:'renderer',
  READY:'ready'
});
window.ThreeDLiteBootPhase=ThreeDLiteBootPhase;

const ThreeDLiteLifecycle=Object.freeze({
  DECLARED:'declared',
  REGISTERING:'registering',
  EDITOR_BOOTING:'editor-booting',
  EDITOR_READY:'editor-ready',
  RENDERER_BOOTING:'renderer-booting',
  READY:'ready',
  DEGRADED:'degraded',
  FAILED:'failed'
});
window.ThreeDLiteLifecycle=ThreeDLiteLifecycle;

const ThreeDLiteAppContext=Object.seal({
  lifecycle:'declared',
  phase:'declaration',
  services:Object.create(null),
  failures:[],
  register(name,service){
    if(!name)throw new Error('AppContext service name required');
    if(this.services[name]&&this.services[name]!==service)throw new Error(`AppContext service already registered: ${name}`);
    this.services[name]=service;
    return service;
  },
  get(name){return this.services[name]||null;},
  require(name){
    const service=this.get(name);
    if(!service)throw new Error(`Required service unavailable: ${name}`);
    return service;
  },
  setLifecycle(lifecycle,phase=this.phase){
    this.lifecycle=lifecycle;
    this.phase=phase;
    document.documentElement.dataset.appLifecycle=lifecycle;
    document.documentElement.dataset.appPhase=phase;
    return lifecycle;
  },
  fail(stage,error){
    const entry=Object.freeze({stage,message:String(error?.message||error),time:Date.now()});
    this.failures.push(entry);
    return entry;
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteAppContext=ThreeDLiteAppContext;

window.SelectionManager=SelectionManager;
window.ToolManager=ToolManager;
window.InputController=InputController;
window.MeshWireManager=MeshWireManager;
window.OverlayManager=OverlayManager;
window.RendererBootstrap=RendererBootstrap;

  hoverEdge(){
    return this.ensure('subHoverEdge','lines',()=>new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
      color:COLORS.hover,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false

      color:COLORS.hover,transparent:true,opacity:.50,
      depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide
    }),90);
  }

function validateArchitectureCore(){
  const errors=[];
  const required={SelectionManager,InputController,ToolManager,CommandSystem,HistoryManager,MeshValidator,MeshOps,DerivedMeshCache,Evaluator,ViewportDisplay,ThreeViewportBackend,PickingSystem,MaterialManager,UVWSystem,ParameterSystem,TransformSystem,TransformHitPolicy,PivotManager,SnapManager,DragSolver,SceneModel};
  for(const [name,value] of Object.entries(required))if(!value)errors.push(`missing ${name}`);
  if(typeof syncTransformTarget!=='function')errors.push('missing syncTransformTarget');
  if(typeof updateDisplayForMesh!=='function')errors.push('missing updateDisplayForMesh');
  if(typeof MeshWireManager==='undefined')errors.push('missing MeshWireManager');
  if(typeof OverlayManager==='undefined')errors.push('missing OverlayManager');
  if(typeof MNMesh!=='function'||typeof MNEdge!=='function'||typeof MNFace!=='function')errors.push('missing MNMesh kernel');
  if(typeof DerivedObject!=='function'||typeof SceneNode!=='function')errors.push('missing object pipeline');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  VIEWPORT:'viewport',
  RENDERER:'renderer',
  READY:'ready'
});
window.ThreeDLiteBootPhase=ThreeDLiteBootPhase;

const ThreeDLiteLifecycle=Object.freeze({
  DECLARED:'declared',
  REGISTERING:'registering',
  EDITOR_BOOTING:'editor-booting',
  EDITOR_READY:'editor-ready',
  RENDERER_BOOTING:'renderer-booting',
  READY:'ready',
  DEGRADED:'degraded',
  FAILED:'failed'
});
window.ThreeDLiteLifecycle=ThreeDLiteLifecycle;

const ThreeDLiteAppContext=Object.seal({
  lifecycle:'declared',
  phase:'declaration',
  services:Object.create(null),
  failures:[],
  register(name,service){
    if(!name)throw new Error('AppContext service name required');
    if(this.services[name]&&this.services[name]!==service)throw new Error(`AppContext service already registered: ${name}`);
    this.services[name]=service;
    return service;
  },
  get(name){return this.services[name]||null;},
  require(name){
    const service=this.get(name);
    if(!service)throw new Error(`Required service unavailable: ${name}`);
    return service;
  },
  setLifecycle(lifecycle,phase=this.phase){
    this.lifecycle=lifecycle;
    this.phase=phase;
    document.documentElement.dataset.appLifecycle=lifecycle;
    document.documentElement.dataset.appPhase=phase;
    return lifecycle;
  },
  fail(stage,error){
    const entry=Object.freeze({stage,message:String(error?.message||error),time:Date.now()});
    this.failures.push(entry);
    return entry;
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteAppContext=ThreeDLiteAppContext;

function register3DLiteCoreServices(){
  ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.REGISTERING,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.REGISTRATION);
  const services={
    SelectionManager:window.SelectionManager||null,
    ToolManager:window.ToolManager||null,
    InputController:window.InputController||null,
    MeshWireManager:window.MeshWireManager||null,
    OverlayManager:window.OverlayManager||null,
    LiteTraceServices:window.LiteTraceServices||null,
    LiteTraceDiagnostics:window.LiteTraceDiagnostics||null,
    RendererBootstrap:window.RendererBootstrap||null,
    SceneGraph:window.ThreeDLiteSceneGraph||null,
    CommandService:window.ThreeDLiteCommandService||null,
    HistoryService:window.ThreeDLiteHistoryService||null,
    NotificationService:window.ThreeDLiteNotificationService||null,
    EditorServices:window.ThreeDLiteEditorServices||null,
    SelectionService:window.ThreeDLiteSelectionService||null,
    ToolService:window.ThreeDLiteToolService||null,
    InputService:window.ThreeDLiteInputService||null,
    TransformService:window.ThreeDLiteTransformService||null,
    PickingService:window.ThreeDLitePickingService||null,
    ViewportService:window.ThreeDLiteViewportService||null,

  }

}

function freezeRenderSnapshot(v){
  if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||Object.isFrozen(v)||ArrayBuffer.isView(v))return v;
  Object.freeze(v);for(const k of Object.keys(v))freezeRenderSnapshot(v[k]);return v;
}

    this.bounds=freezeRenderSnapshot(d.bounds);Object.freeze(this);
  }

  keys:Object.freeze(['sceneRevision','geometryRevision','materialRevision','lightRevision','cameraRevision']),

      scene:p.sceneRevision!==c.sceneRevision,
      geometry:p.geometryRevision!==c.geometryRevision,
      material:p.materialRevision!==c.materialRevision,
      light:p.lightRevision!==c.lightRevision,
      camera:p.cameraRevision!==c.cameraRevision
    };

var RenderCoreA=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.03-core-a',
  createRequest(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const job=RenderFramework.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
    const scene=RenderSceneBoundary.buildSnapshot();
    const detached=RenderSceneBoundary.verifyDetached(scene);
    if(!detached.ok)throw new Error(detached.errors.join('; '));
    job.renderScene=scene;
    return job;
  },
  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g1=validateRenderGroup1();if(!g1.ok)errors.push(...g1.errors);
    const g2=validateRenderGroup2();if(!g2.ok)errors.push(...g2.errors);
    return {ok:!errors.length,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreA=RenderCoreA;

function validateRenderGroup2(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSceneBoundary.buildSnapshot!=='function')errors.push('Missing buildSnapshot');
  if(typeof RenderSceneBoundary.verifyDetached!=='function')errors.push('Missing detached verification');
  if(typeof RenderSnapshotValidator.validate!=='function')errors.push('Missing snapshot validator');
  if(typeof RenderRevisionPolicy.invalidation!=='function')errors.push('Missing revision invalidation policy');
  return {ok:!errors.length,errors};
}

class RenderGeometryPacket{
  constructor(data){
    this.objectId=data.objectId;
    this.name=data.name;
    this.positions=data.positions;
    this.normals=data.normals;
    this.uvs=data.uvs;
    this.indices=data.indices;
    this.materialIndices=data.materialIndices;
    this.bounds=freezeRenderSnapshot(data.bounds);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

const RenderGeometryValidation=Object.freeze({
  validatePacket(packet){
    const errors=[];
    if(!packet)errors.push('Missing geometry packet');
    const p=packet?.positions,i=packet?.indices,n=packet?.normals,u=packet?.uvs,m=packet?.materialIndices;
    if(!ArrayBuffer.isView(p)||p.length%3)errors.push('Malformed position buffer');
    if(!ArrayBuffer.isView(i)||i.length%3)errors.push('Malformed index buffer');
    const vc=(p?.length||0)/3,tc=(i?.length||0)/3;
    if(n?.length&&n.length!==p.length)errors.push('Normal count mismatch');
    if(u?.length&&u.length!==vc*2)errors.push('UV count mismatch');
    if(m?.length!==tc)errors.push('Material-index count mismatch');
    if(p)for(let k=0;k<p.length;k++)if(!Number.isFinite(p[k])){errors.push('Non-finite position');break}
    if(n)for(let k=0;k<n.length;k++)if(!Number.isFinite(n[k])){errors.push('Non-finite normal');break}
    if(u)for(let k=0;k<u.length;k++)if(!Number.isFinite(u[k])){errors.push('Non-finite UV');break}
    if(i)for(let k=0;k<i.length;k++)if(!Number.isInteger(i[k])||i[k]<0||i[k]>=vc){errors.push('Index out of range');break}
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors,vertexCount:vc,triangleCount:tc};
  }
});

var RenderGeometryCompiler={
  lastStats:Object.freeze({objectCount:0,vertexCount:0,inputTriangles:0}),
  compileObject(renderObject){
    const m=renderObject?.mesh;if(!m)return null;
    const packet=new RenderGeometryPacket({
      objectId:renderObject.id,name:renderObject.name,
      positions:new Float32Array(m.positions),normals:new Float32Array(m.normals),uvs:new Float32Array(m.uvs),
      indices:new Uint32Array(m.indices),materialIndices:new Uint16Array(m.materialIndices),
      bounds:{min:[...m.bounds.min],max:[...m.bounds.max]}
    });
    const check=RenderGeometryValidation.validatePacket(packet);
    if(!check.ok)throw new GeometryCompileError(`${renderObject.name||renderObject.id}: ${check.errors.join('; ')}`);
    return packet;
  },
  compileScene(renderScene){
    const packets=[];let vertexCount=0,inputTriangles=0;

      const p=this.compileObject(obj);if(!p)continue;
      packets.push(p);vertexCount+=p.positions.length/3;inputTriangles+=p.indices.length/3;
    }
    this.lastStats=Object.freeze({objectCount:packets.length,vertexCount,inputTriangles});
    return packets;
  },
  transferables(packet){return [packet.positions.buffer,packet.normals.buffer,packet.uvs.buffer,packet.indices.buffer,packet.mater

self.onmessage=e=>{
  const m=e.data||{};
  try{
    if(m.type==='cancel'){
      cancelledJobs.add(m.jobId);
      return;
    }
    if(m.type==='pause'){
      paused=true;
      return;
    }
    if(m.type==='resume'){
      paused=false;
      return;
    }
    if(m.type==='reset'){
      paused=false;
      cancelledJobs.clear();
      return;
    }
    if(m.type==='prepareGeometry'){
      if(paused){
        self.postMessage({type:'paused',jobId:m.jobId,taskId:m.taskId});
        return;
      }
      const r=prepareGeometry(m);
      const transfer=[];
      if(r.bounds)transfer.push(r.bounds.buffer);
      if(r.triangleIndices)transfer.push(r.triangleIndices.buffer);
      self.postMessage(r,transfer);
    }
  }catch(err){
    self.postMessage({
      type:'workerError',
      jobId:m.jobId,
      taskId:m.taskId,
      message:String(err&&err.message||err)
    });
  }
};

var RenderWorkerPool = class RenderWorkerPool{
  constructor(size=0){
    const hc=Math.max(1,Number(navigator.hardwareConcurrency)||2);
    this.autoSize=Math.max(1,Math.min(Math.max(1,hc-1),64));
    this.size=this._normalizeSize(size||this.autoSize);
    this.workers=[];
    this.idle=[];
    this.queue=[];
    this.activeTasks=new Map();
    this.nextTaskId=1;
    this.nextWorkerId=1;
    this.workerURL=null;
    this.available=typeof Worker!=='undefined';
    this.disposed=false;
    this.paused=false;
    this.generation=1;
    this.failureCount=0;
    this.completedCount=0;
    this.cancelledCount=0;
    this.timeoutCount=0;
  }

  _normalizeSize(value){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(Number(value)||1,64));
  }

  _ensureURL(){
    if(this.workerURL||!this.available)return;
    const blob=new Blob([RenderWorkerKernelSource],{type:'text/javascript'});
    this.workerURL=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  }

  _spawnWorker(){
    if(this.disposed||!this.available)return null;
    this._ensureURL();
    const worker=new Worker(this.workerURL);
    worker.__renderWorkerId=this.nextWorkerId++;
    worker.__renderBusy=false;
    worker.__renderState=this.paused?'paused':'idle';
    worker.__renderTaskId=null;
    worker.__renderJobId=null;
    worker.__renderPoolGeneration=this.generation;
    worker.__perf={tasks:0,totalMs:0,lastMs:0,failures:0,idleSince:performance.now(),busySince:0};
    worker.onmessage=e=>this._handleMessage(worker,e.data||{});
    worker.onerror=e=>{
      e?.preventDefault?.();
      this._handleFailure(worker,e?.message||'Worker error');
    };
    this.workers.push(worker);
    if(!this.paused)this.idle.push(worker);
    return worker;
  }

  _retireWorker(worker,{terminate=true,replace=false}={}){
    if(!worker)return;
    this.idle=this.idle.filter(w=>w!==worker);
    this.workers=this.workers.filter(w=>w!==worker);
    worker.__renderBusy=false;
    worker.__renderState='retired';
    worker.__renderTaskId=null;
    worker.__renderJobId=null;
    if(terminate){try{worker.terminate();}catch(_){}}
    if(replace&&!this.disposed&&this.available&&this.workers.length<this.size){
      this._spawnWorker();
    }
  }

  ensure(){
    if(this.disposed||!this.available)return;
    this._ensureURL();
    while(this.workers.length<this.size)this._spawnWorker();
    while(this.workers.length>this.size){
      const idleWorker=this.idle.pop();
      if(idleWorker)this._retireWorker(idleWorker,{terminate:true,replace:false});
      else break; // active workers retire naturally after their current task
    }
  }

  setSize(size){
    const next=this._normalizeSize(size);
    if(next===this.size)return this.

  async prepare(job,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(job.renderScene);
    if(!packets.length)return new PreparedGeometrySet([]);

    const items=[];
    const total=packets.length;

    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});

      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled){
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,25));
      }

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;

      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){
          result=this.mainThread(job,packet);
        }else{
          throw err;
        }
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));

      onProgress((i+1)/total,items[items.length-1]);
    }

    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  }
};


/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — CORE B
   CLEAN GROUP 3 + GROUP 4 CONSOLIDATION
   ============================================================ */
class RenderExecutionGeometry{
  constructor(renderScene){
    this.accelerationPackets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
    this.workerPackets=this.accelerationPackets.map(p=>new RenderGeometryPacket({
      objectId:p.objectId,name:p.name,
      positions:new Float32Array(p.positions),
      normals:new Float32Array(p.normals),
      uvs:new Float32Array(p.uvs),
      indices:new Uint32Array(p.indices),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array(p.materialIndices),
      bounds:{min:[...p.bounds.min],max:[...p.bounds.max]}
    }));
    Object.freeze(this.accelerationPackets);
    Object.freeze(this.workerPackets);
  }
}

var RenderCoreB=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.16-core-b-part2',

  createExecutionGeometry(renderScene){
    return new RenderExecutionGeometry(renderScene);
  },

  buildAccelerationFromPackets(packets){
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
    store.finalize();
    return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
  },

  async prepareWorkerGeometry(job,pool,executionGeometry,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const items=[],packets=executionGeometry.workerPackets,total=packets.length;
    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,25));

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;
      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){job.progress.workerFallback=true;result=RenderGeometryPreparation.mainThread(job,packet);}
        else throw err;
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));
      onProgress((i+1)/Math.max(1,total),items[items.length-1]);
    }
    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g3=validateRenderGroup3();if(!g3.ok)errors.push(...g3.errors);
    const g4=validateRenderGroup4();if(!g4.ok)errors.push(...g4.errors);

/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — GROUP 4
   Rays + Triangle Intersections + BVH + Camera

const RayMath=Object.freeze({
  EPSILON:1e-6,
  add(a,b){return [a[0]+b[0],a[1]+b[1],a[2]+b[2]]},
  sub(a,b){return [a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1],a[2]-b[2]]},
  mul(a,s){return [a[0]*s,a[1]*s,a[2]*s]},
  dot(a,b){return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]},
  cross(a,b){return [a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]]},
  length(a){return Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])},
  normalize(a){const l=Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])||1;return [a[0]/l,a[1]/l,a[2]/l]},
  min3(a,b){return [Math.min(a[0],b[0]),Math.min(a[1],b[1]),Math.min(a[2],b[2])]},
  max3(a,b){return [Math.max(a[0],b[0]),Math.max(a[1],b[1]),Math.max(a[2],b[2])]}
});

const RenderRayType=Object.freeze({CAMERA:'camera',SHADOW:'shadow',DIFFUSE:'diffuse',GLOSSY:'glossy',TRANSMISSION:'transmission'});
class RenderRay{
  constructor(origin,direction,tMin=1e-5,tMax=Infinity,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA,depth=0){
    this.origin=origin;this.direction=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    this.invDirection=[
      Math.abs(this.direction[0])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[0],
      Math.abs(this.direction[1])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[1],
      Math.abs(this.direction[2])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[2]
    ];
    this.tMin=tMin;this.tMax=tMax;this.type=type;this.depth=depth;
  }
  at(t){return RayMath.add(this.origin,RayMath.mul(this.direction,t))}
}
const RenderRayPolicy=Object.freeze({
  epsilonForPoint(p){const scale=Math.max(1,Math.abs(p[0]),Math.abs(p[1]),Math.abs(p[2]));return Math.min(1e-2,Math.max(1e-6,scale*1e-7));}
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderRayType,RenderRayPolicy});

class RenderHit{
  constructor(){
    this.hit=false;this.t=Infinity;this.u=0;this.v=0;
    this.objectIndex=-1;this.triangleIndex=-1;
    this.position=null;this.geometricNormal=null;this.orientedGeometricNormal=null;this.frontFace=true;this.materialIndex=0;
  }
}

const TriangleIntersector=Object.freeze({
  intersect(ray,p0,p1,p2,hit=new RenderHit()){
    const e1=RayMath.sub(p1,p0),e2=RayMath.sub(p2,p0);
    const pvec=RayMath.cross(ray.direction,e2);
    const det=RayMath.dot(e1,pvec);
    if(Math.abs(det)<RayMath.EPSILON)return hit;
    const invDet=1/det;
    const tvec=RayMath.sub(ray.origin,p0);
    const u=RayMath.dot(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
    if(u<0||u>1)return hit;
    const qvec=RayMath.cross(tvec,e1);
    const v=RayMath.dot(ray.direction,qvec)*invDet;
    if(v<0||u+v>1)return hit;
    const t=RayMath.dot(e2,qvec)*invDet;
    if(t<ray.tMin||t>ray.tMax||t>=hit.t)return hit;
    hit.hit=true;hit.t=t;hit.u=u;hit.v=v;
    hit.position=ray.at(t);
    hit.geometricNormal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(e1,e2));
    hit.frontFace=RayMath.dot(ray.direction,hit.geometricNormal)<0;
    hit.orientedGeometricNormal=hit.frontFace?hit.geometricNormal:RayMath.mul(hit.geometricNormal,-1);
    return hit;
  }
});

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderColor=Object.freeze({
  clamp01(x){return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x))},
  add(a,b){return [a[0]+b[0],a[1]+b[1],a[2]+b[2]]},
  mul(a,b){
    if(Array.isArray(b))return [a[0]*b[0],a[1]*b[1],a[2]*b[2]];
    return [a[0]*b,a[1]*b,a[2]*b];
  },
  mix(a,b,t){return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t,a[2]+(b[2]-a[2])*t]},
  srgbToLinear1(x){
    x=this.clamp01(x);return x<=.04045?x/12.92:Math.pow((x+.055)/1.055,2.4);
  },
  linearToSrgb1(x){
    x=Math.max(0,x);return x<=.0031308?12.92*x:1.055*Math.pow(x,1/2.4)-.055;
  },
  srgbToLinear(c){return [this.srgbToLinear1(c[0]),this.srgbToLinear1(c[1]),this.srgbToLinear1(c[2])]},
  linearToSrgb(c){return [this.linearToSrgb1(c[0]),this.linearToSrgb1(c[1]),this.linearToSrgb1(c[2])]},
  hexToLinear(hex){
    const h=String(hex||'#202020').replace('#','');
    const n=parseInt(h.length===3?h.split('').map(x=>x+x).join(''):h,16);
    if(!Number.isFinite(n))return [0.0144,0.0144,0.0144];
    return this.srgbToLinear([((n>>16)&255)/255,((n>>8)&255)/255,(n&255)/255]);
  },
  aces(c){
    const f=x=>{
      const a=2.51,b=.03,d=2.43,e=.59,g=.14;
      return this.clamp01((x*(a*x+b))/(x*(d*x+e)+g));
    };
    return [f(c[0]),f(c[1]),f(c[2])];
  }
});

const RenderMaterialPolicy=Object.freeze({
  finiteColor(c,fallback=[.54,.54,.54]){
    if(!Array.isArray(c)||c.length<3)return fallback.slice();
    return c.slice(0,3).map((v,i)=>{
      v=Number(v);
      return Number.isFinite(v)?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v)):fallback[i];
    });
  },
  scalar(v,fallback,min,max){
    v=Number(v);
    if(!Number.isFinite(v))v=fallback;
    return Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));
  },
  shading(v){
    return ['physical','lambert','unlit'].includes(v)?v:'physical';
  }
});

const RenderPerformancePolicy16=Object.freeze({
  tileSize(job){
    const raw=String(job?.settings?.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(raw==='Auto'){
      const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
      const w=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||640);
      const h=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||360);
      const pixels=w*h;
      const threads=Math.max(1,Number(job?.progress?.threads)||Math.max(1,(navigator.hardwareConcurrency||2)-1));
      let size=q==='Draft'?32:q==='Ultra'?8:16;
      if(pixels>1920*1080&&threads>=8)size=Math.max(size,32);
      if(pixels<640*480)size=Math.min(size,16);
      if(threads<=2)size=Math.max(size,32);
      return size;
    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}

  constructor(){
    this.cameraRays=0;

    const totalRays=this.cameraRays+this.pathRays;
    return Object.freeze({
      cameraRays:this.cameraRays,
      pathRays:this.pathRays,
      shadowRays:this.shadowRays,
      probeRays:this.probeRays,
      totalRays:totalRays+this.shadowRays+this.probeRays,
      samples:this.samples,
      yields:this.yields,
      raysPerSecond:Math.round(totalRays/(elapsed/1000)),
      stageMs:Object.freeze({...this.stageMs})
    });
  }
}

    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});

  v3a:new Float64Array(3),v3b:new Float64Array(3),v3c:new Float64Array(3),rgb:new Float64Array(3)
});
window.LiteTraceScratch=LiteTraceScratch;

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

var RenderPerformanceCounters16 = class RenderPerformanceCounters16{
  constructor(){
    this.cameraRays=0;
    this.pathRays=0;
    this.shadowRays=0;
    this.probeRays=0;
    this.samples=0;
    this.yields=0;
    this.stageMs={snapshot:0,geometry:0,worker:0,bvh:0,shading:0,lightCache:0,render:0,denoise:0,toneMap:0,post:0};
    this.startedAt=RenderProfiler16.now();
  }
  addRay(type){
    if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;
    else if(type===RenderRayType.SHADOW)this.shadowRays++;
    else this.pathRays++;
  }
  addProbe(){this.probeRays++;}
  markStage(name,start){
    return RenderProfiler16.mark(this,name,start);
  }
  snapshot(){
    const elapsed=Math.max(.001,performance.now()-this.startedAt);
    const totalRays=this.cameraRays+this.pathRays;
    return Object.freeze({
      cameraRays:this.cameraRays,
      pathRays:this.pathRays,
      shadowRays:this.shadowRays,
      probeRays:this.probeRays,
      totalRays:totalRays+this.shadowRays+this.probeRays,
      samples:this.samples,
      yields:this.yields,
      raysPerSecond:Math.round(totalRays/(elapsed/1000)),
      stageMs:Object.freeze({...this.stageMs})
    });
  }
}

const RenderPerformanceRuntime16=Object.freeze({
  attach(job){
    if(!job.performanceState)job.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    return job.performanceState;
  },
  markStage(job,name,start){
    this.attach(job).markStage(name,start);
  },
  snapshot(job){
    return job?.performanceState?.snapshot?.()||null;
  }
});

const RenderPerformancePolicy16=Object.freeze({
  tileSize(job){
    const raw=String(job?.settings?.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(raw==='Auto'){
      const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
      const w=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||640);
      const h=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||360);
      const pixels=w*h;
      const threads=Math.max(1,Number(job?.progress?.threads)||Math.max(1,(navigator.hardwareConcurrency||2)-1));
      let size=q==='Draft'?32:q==='Ultra'?8:16;
      if(pixels>1920*1080&&threads>=8)size=Math.max(size,32);
      if(pixels<640*480)size=Math.min(size,16);
      if(threads<=2)size=Math.max(size,32);
      return size;
    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});

window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const compiled=RenderPart4Core.compile(job);
    const backgroundFallback=RenderColor.hexToLinear(job.settings.background||'#202020');
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const primaryBatch16=new RenderRayBatch16(Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)));
    const rowChunk=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));
    await RenderTileScheduler16.runLocal(job,tiles16,async tile=>{
      for(let y=tile.y;y<tile.y+tile.height;y++){
        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreC=RenderCoreC;

const RenderGroup5Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'render material compilation',
    'linear color conversion',
    'GGX physical BRDF',
    'basic direct directional lighting',
    'basic environment fill',
    'tone mapping and display conversion',
    'barycentric shading-normal interpolation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core A material snapshots',
    'Core B ray/BVH hit data',
    'Core A render settings'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor viewport materials',
    'full scene light object system',
    'shadow visibility',
    'area-light sampling',
    'path GI',
    'light cache',
    'denoising'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],

var EditorLightSystem={
  nextId:1,
  isLightObject(obj){return !!obj?.userData?.renderLight},

  iconGeometry(type,size){
    const s=Math.max(2,Math.min(30,Number(size)||10));
    if(type==='rectangle')return new THREE.BoxGeometry(s*1.4,s*.08,s);
    if(type==='disc')return new THREE.CylinderGeometry(s*.55,s*.55,s*.08,16);
    if(type==='sphere'||type==='dome')return new THREE.SphereGeometry(s*.45,10,6);
    if(type==='spot')return new THREE.ConeGeometry(s*.35,s*.8,12);
    if(type==='sun')return new THREE.CylinderGeometry(s*.45,s*.45,s*.08,16);
    if(type==='mesh')return new THREE.BoxGeometry(s*.8,s*.8,s*.08);
    return new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(s*.4,0);
  },

  create(type,opts={}){
    type=LightTypes.includes(type)?type:'point';

      enabled:opts.enabled!==false,

      range:Math.max(0,Number(opts.range??d.range??0)),

      height:Math.max(.01,Number(opts.height??d.height??size*2)),

      penumbra:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(opts.penumbra??d.penumbra??.15))),
      castShadows:opts.castShadows!==false,
      renderOnly:true
    };

   Shadows + Area Sampling + Environment
