const RenderSampling=Object.freeze({
  hash32(x){
    x=(x|0)+0x6D2B79F5|0;
    x=Math.imul(x^(x>>>15),x|1);
    x^=x+Math.imul(x^(x>>>7),x|61);
    return ((x^(x>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;
  },
  sample2D(seed,index){
    return [
      this.hash32((seed*73856093)^(index*19349663)),
      this.hash32((seed*83492791)^(index*2971215073))
    ];
  },
  concentricDisk(u1,u2){
    const sx=2*u1-1,sy=2*u2-1;
    if(sx===0&&sy===0)return [0,0];
    let r,theta;
    if(Math.abs(sx)>Math.abs(sy)){
      r=sx;theta=(Math.PI/4)*(sy/sx);
    }else{
      r=sy;theta=(Math.PI/2)-(Math.PI/4)*(sx/sy);
    }
    return [r*Math.cos(theta),r*Math.sin(theta)];
  }
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,light.intensity);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=1/(dist*dist);
    if(light.range>0){
      const f=Math.max(0,1-dist/light.range);
      attenuation*=f*f;
    }

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:RenderColor.mul(light.color,light.intensity*attenuation*emitterCos/safeArea),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMateria
--- occurrence 39 at 333955 ---

        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);

        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreD=RenderCoreD;

function validateRenderGroup7(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

const GGXImportanceSampler=Object.freeze({
  sampleHalfVector(n,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.045,roughness);
    const a2=a*a;
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u1;
    const cosTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(1-u2)/(1+(a2-1)*u2)));
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta));
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,sinTheta*Math.cos(phi)),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,sinTheta*Math.sin(phi))),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)
      )
    );
  },
  reflect(i,n){return RayMath.sub(i,RayMath.mul(n,2*RayMath.dot(i,n)));},
  distribution(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=Math.max(.045,roughness),a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/(Math.PI*d*d+1e-8);
  }
});

const DielectricTransport=Object.freeze({
  fresnelDielectric(cosI,etaI,etaT){
    cosI=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,cosI));
    let entering=cosI>0;
    if(!entering){const t=etaI;etaI=etaT;etaT=t;cosI=Math.abs(cosI);}
    const sinI=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosI*cosI));
    const sinT=etaI/etaT*sinI;
    if(sinT>=1)return 1;
    const cosT=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-sinT*sinT));
    const rs=((etaT*cosI)-(etaI*cosT))/((etaT*cosI)+(etaI*cosT));
    const rp=((etaI*cosI)-(etaT*cosT))/((etaI*cosI)+(etaT*cosT));
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(rs*rs+rp*rp)*.5));
  },
  refract(i,n,eta){
    const cosi=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,RayMath.dot(i,n)));
    const k=1-eta*eta*(1-cosi*cosi);
    if(k<0)return null;
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(RayMath.mul(i,eta),RayMath.mul(n,eta*cosi+Math.sqrt(k))));
  },
  beerLambert(color,distance,attenuationDistance){
    if(!Number.isFinite(attenuationDistance)||attenuationDistance<=0||distance<=0)return [1,1,1];
    return color.map(c=>Math.pow(Math.max(1e-6,c),distance/attenuationDistance));
  }
});

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  averageSamples(){

    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);

    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

var PathGIRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;
    job.lightTree=RenderCoreH.createLightTree(job,compiled);
    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;
    const accumulator=new ProgressiveAccumulator(width,height);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const primaryMask=new Uint8Array(width*height);
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;
    const maxSamples=Math.max(1,settings.maxSamples||1);
    const progressive=settings.progressive!==false;
    const passLimit=progressive?maxSamples:1;
    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));

      let sampledThisPass=0;
      const activePixels16=progressive?accumulator.compactActive(settings):(()=>{
        const a=new Uint32Array(width*height);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=i;return a;
      })();
      if(activePixels16.length===0)break;

      const workQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16.fromIndices(activePixels16,Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*4));
      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;
          const jitterX=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,20)-.5;
          const jitterY=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,21)-.5;
          const ray=RenderPart5Core.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x+jitterX,rr.y0+y+jitterY,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){
            const ph=sample.primaryHitRecord;
            if(ph?.hit)RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,ph,seed));
            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();
      onProgress(Math.min(1,completedPasses/passLimit));

      if(!progressive)break;
      if(settings.adaptive&&completedPasses>=settings.minSamples&&accumulator.active===0)break;
      if(sampledThisPass===0)break;
    }

    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

      aovs.set3('Beauty',i,beauty);
      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');
      const direct=d?[d[o],d[o+1],d[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      const emission=e?[e[o],e[o+1],e[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      aovs.set3('Global Illumination',i,[
        Math.max(0,beauty[0]-direct[0]-emission[0]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[1]-direct[1]-emission[1]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[2]-direct[2]-emission[2])
      ]);
    }
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
        samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
        passes:completedPasses,
        maxBounces:PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job),
        estimator:'Path GI + direct NEE + power-heuristic MIS',
        mis:true,
        adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
        progressive:!!settings.progressive,
        russianRoulette:true,
        importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture',
        noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
        transmission:true,
        secondaryGI:settings.secondaryGI,
        lightCache:job.lightCache?.stats?.()||null,
        emissiveTriangleLights:compiled.emissiveTriangles?.triangles?.length||0,
        diagnostics:RenderPart5Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

const RenderGroup8Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'multi-bounce path integration',
    'path throughput',
    'secondary-ray spawning',
    'cosine hemisphere BSDF sampling baseline',
    'environment contribution on path miss',
    'path-GI render mode'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B ray/BVH tracing',
    'Core C physical materials and lights',
    'Group 7 shadow visibility and direct-light sampling'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'progressive accumulation',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'MIS',
    'Light Cache',
    'denoising',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

const RenderGroup14Contract=Object.freeze({

    'normalized render-region policy',
    'region-to-camera pixel mapping',
    'last successful render state',
    'render-last replay',

    'interactive render-region selection'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Group 13 final presentation',
    'Group 12 AOV buffers',
    'existing Fast renderer',
    'existing Path renderer',
    'existing framebuffer'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor startup','viewport','selection','transforms','mesh editing',
    'scene evaluation','BVH','materials','lights','path transport'
  ])
});

var RenderRegion14=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(region){
    if(!region)return null;
    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.x)||0));
    const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.y)||0));
    const width=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-x,Number(region.width)||0));
    const height=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-y,Number(region.height)||0));
    if(width<1e-4||height<1e-4)return null;
    return Object.freeze({x,y,width,height});
  },

  resolve(job,maxWidth=640,maxHeight=360){
    const sourceW=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||1);
    const sourceH=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||1);
    const scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/sourceW,maxHeight/sourceH);
    const fullW=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceW*scale));
    const fullH=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceH*scale));
    const r=this.sanitize(job?.region);

    if(!r){
      return Object.freeze({
        region:false,fullW,fullH,
        x0:0,y0:0,width:fullW,height:fullH,
        normalized:null
      });
    }

    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullW-1,Math.floor(r.x*fullW)));
    const y0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullH-1,Math.floor(r.y*fullH)));
    const x1=Math.max(x0+1,Math.min(fullW,Math.ceil((r.x+r.width)*fullW)));
    const y1=Math.max(y0+1,Math.min(fullH,Math.ceil((r.y+r.height)*fullH)));

    return Object.freeze({
      region:true,fullW,fullH,x0,y0,
      width:x1-x0,height:y1-y0,
      normalized:r
    });
  },

  mapNested(base,local){
    const b=this.sanitize(base);
    const l=this.sanitize(local);
    if(!l)return null;
    if(!b)return l;
    return this.sanitize({
      x:b.x+l.x*b.width,
      y:b.y+l.y*b.height,
      width:l.width*b.width,
      height:l.height*b.height
    });
  }
});

function validateRenderCoreH(){

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

      }catch(err){errors.push(`${name}: ${String(err?.message||err)}`);}

        return r;
      }
      RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
      return {ok:true,errors:[]};
    }catch(err){
      RendererRuntimeState.fail('bootstrap',err);
      return {ok:false,errors:[String(err?.message||err)]};
    }
  },

  },

const RenderPart3Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'worker lifecycle','worker queue','worker cancellation',
    'worker timeout recovery','worker resizing','worker diagnostics'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze(['immutable render packets','render job IDs','CPU thread setting']),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze(['editor scene','editor geometry','viewport','selection','undo','materials'])
});

function runRenderPart3SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  try{
    const pool=new RenderWorkerPool(2);
    if(pool.size!==2)errors.push('Worker pool explicit size failed');
    if(pool.setSize(99)!==64)errors.push('Worker pool upper clamp failed');
    if(pool.setSize(0)!==1)errors.push('Worker pool lower clamp failed');
    const st=pool.stats();
    if(!('queued' in st&&'active' in st&&'failures' in st&&'timeouts' in st))
      errors.push('Worker statistics incomplete');
    pool.disposed=true;
  }catch(err){errors.push('Worker pool structural test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  if(typeof RenderJobController.prototype.configureThreads!=='function')errors.push('Controller thread configuration missing');
  if(typeof RenderJobController.prototype.workerStats!=='function')errors.push('Controller worker statistics missing');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderPart3(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool!=='function')errors.push('RenderWorkerPool missing');
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool.prototype.setSize!=='function')errors.push('Worker resizing missing');
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool.prototype.cancelJob!=='function')errors.push('Worker cancellation missing');
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool.prototype.reset!=='function')errors.push('Worker recovery/reset missing');
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool.prototype.stats!=='function')errors.push('Worker diagnostics missing');
  const t=runRenderPart3SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

var RenderPart3Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.17-part3-gen3',
  configure(controller,threadSetting){
    if(!controller?.workerPool)throw new WorkerError('RenderJobController worker pool unavailable');
    return controller.configureThreads(threadSetting);
  },
  cancel(controller,jobId){return controller?.workerPool?.cancelJob?.(jobId)||0;},
  recover(controller){
    if(!controller?.workerPool)throw new WorkerError('Worker pool unavailable for recovery');
    return controller.workerPool.reset();
  },
  stats(controller){return controller?.workerPool?.stats?.()||null;},
  validate:validateRenderPart3
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderPart3Contract,runRenderPart3SelfTests,validateRenderPart3,RenderPart3Core});

const FastRenderDiagnostics=Object.freeze({
  summarize(job,compiled,acceleration){
    const types={};
    for(const l of (compiled?.lights||[]))types[l.type]=(types[l.type]||0)+1;
    return Object.freeze({
      renderer:'Fast Direct',
      lights:(compiled?.lights||[]).length,
      lightTypes:Object.freeze(types),
      materials:compiled?.materials?.list?.length||0,
      triangles:acceleration?.store?.triangleCount?.()||0,
      bvh:acceleration?.bvh?.stats||null,
      globalIllumination:false
    });
  }
});

function runRenderPart4SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const finiteColor=c=>Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);

  try{
    const p=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-point',name:'Point',type:'point',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:100,range:5,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:0,
      castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const smp=DirectLightSampler.sample(p,[0,0,0]);
    if(!finiteColor(smp.radiance)||smp.radiance.some(v=>v!==0))errors.push('Point range cutoff failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Point light test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const spot=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-spot',name:'Spot',type:'spot',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:10,range:100,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:20,penumbra:.1,
      castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const outside=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[10,0,0]);
    if(!finiteColor(outside.radiance))errors.push('Spot radiance non-finite');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Spot test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const rect=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-rect',name:'Rect',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:10,range:0,size:1,width:.01,height:.01,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,
      castShadows:true,position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const front=AreaLightSampler.sample(rect,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    const back=AreaLightSampler.sample(rect,[0,0,10],.5,.5);
    if(!finiteColor(front.radiance)||front.area<RenderLightPolicy.minArea)errors.push('Rectangle safe-area test fail
--- occurrence 67 at 529045 ---
ization test failed');
    }
  }catch(err){errors.push('Light sanitization test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderPart4(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderLightPolicy?.area!=='function')errors.push('RenderLightPolicy missing');
  if(typeof SceneLightCompiler?.compile!=='function')errors.push('SceneLightCompiler missing');
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility?.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler?.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler?.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer?.render!=='function')errors.push('Fast renderer missing');
  if(typeof FastRenderDiagnostics?.summarize!=='function')errors.push('Fast render diagnostics missing');
  const t=runRenderPart4SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  version:'3.79.18-part4-gen3',
  compile(job){return job.shadingContext||RenderCoreC.compileScene(job.renderScene,job.settings);},

function validateRenderPart5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderMaterialPolicy?.finiteColor!=='function')errors.push('RenderMaterialPolicy missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF?.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator?.normal!=='function')errors.push('Surface normal interpolator missing');
  if(typeof RenderCameraPolicy?.sanitize!=='function')errors.push('RenderCameraPolicy missing');
  if(typeof RenderCameraRayGenerator?.ray!=='function')errors.push('Render camera ray generator missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator?.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer?.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const t=runRenderPart5SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function runRenderPart6SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      engine:'bad',quality:'bad',width:-10,height:999999,percentage:999,
      maxBounces:99,background:'bad',environmentStrength:-1,cpuThreads:'999',
      progressive:true,adaptive:true,denoise:'Quality',format:'EXR',colorDepth:'32-bit Float'
    });
    if(s.engine!=='fast'||s.width!==16||s.height!==8192||s.percentage!==200)errors.push('Settings numeric sanitization failed');
    if(s.maxBounces!==16||!s.progressive||!s.adaptive||s.denoise!=='Quality')errors.push('Deferred setting sanitization failed');
    if(s.format!=='PNG'||s.colorDepth!=='8-bit'||s.cpuThreads!=='16')errors.push('Supported output/thread sanitization failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Settings policy test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=2;canvas.height=2;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray([255,0,0,255,0,255,0,255,0,0,255,255,255,255,255,255]);

    if(!fb.hasValidFrame||fb.snapshot()?.rgba?.length!==16)errors.push('Framebuffer presentation/snapshot failed');
    fb.clear();
    if(fb.hasValidFrame||fb.snapshot()!==null)errors.push('Framebuffer stale-frame invalidation failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Framebuffer test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{

    RenderPerformanceMetrics.begin(j);

const ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext=Object.freeze({
  create(job){
    if(!job)throw new Error('RenderExecutionContext requires a render job');
    return Object.seal({
      job,
      scheduler:ThreeDLiteRenderSchedulerService.implementation(),
      workers:ThreeDLiteWorkerService.implementation(),
      acceleration:ThreeDLiteAccelerationService.implementation(),
      performanceState:ThreeDLitePerformanceService.attach(job),
      createdAt:globalThis.performance?.now?.()??Date.now()
    });
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext=ThreeDLiteRenderExecutionContext;

  get scheduler(){return ThreeDLiteRenderSchedulerService},

  get performance(){return ThreeDLitePerformanceService},

function get3DLiteRenderExecutionSnapshot(job=null){

  return Object.freeze({
    jobId:j?.id??null,
    workerAvailable:ThreeDLiteWorkerService.available(),
    schedulerAvailable:!!ThreeDLiteRenderSchedulerService.implementation(),
    accelerationAvailable:!!ThreeDLiteAccelerationService.implementation(),
    samplingAvailable:!!ThreeDLiteSamplingService.implementation(),
    performanceAvailable:!!ThreeDLitePerformanceService.implementation(),
    hasPerformanceState:!!ThreeDLitePerformanceService.metrics(j)
  });
}

      lastCompletedSettings:RenderFramework?.lastCompletedSettings||null

function runFinalRendererOwnershipChecks(){
  const failures=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)failures.push(m);};

    expect(PathGIRenderer.render.toString().includes('RenderCoreH.createPathGuide'),'Path renderer bypasses Core H Path Guide');
    expect(PathGIRenderer.render.toString().includes('RenderCoreH.attachPerformance'),'Path renderer bypasses Core H performance');
    expect(BasicDirectRenderer.render.toString().includes('RenderCoreH.attachPerformance'),'Fast renderer bypasses Core H performance');

    expect(RenderFramework.render.toString().includes('RendererMasterCore.createRequest'),'RenderFramework bypasses renderer master');
    expect(RenderFramework.showChannel.toString().includes('RenderPresenter13.frame'),'Final presentation bypasses Group 13 presenter');
    expect(RenderFramework.renderRegion.toString().includes('RenderRegion14'),'Render Region bypasses Group 14 policy');
    expect(RenderFramework.renderLast.toString().includes('lastCompleted'),'Render Last does not use successful render state');

    expect(!RenderCoreHContract.rejects.includes('editor startup'),'Core H startup ownership rule missing');
  }catch(e){failures.push('Ownership exception: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:failures.length===0,failures};
}

function runFinalRendererFeatureChecks(){
  const failures=[];
  const collect=(name,fn)=>{
    try{
      const r=fn();
      if(!r?.ok){
        const list=r?.failures||r?.errors||['unknown failure'];
        list.forEach(x=>failures.push(name+': '+x));
      }
    }catch(e){failures.push(name+': '+String(e?.message||e));}
  };

  collect('Existing release gate',()=>runRendererReleaseGate());
  collect('Consolidation checks',()=>runRende
--- occurrence 85 at 559240 ---
or.'
  });

  window.__3DLiteFinalRendererRelease=result;
  return result;
}

/* Low viewport debug line — diagnostics only, never blocks editor startup. */
var ViewportDebugLine=Object.freeze({
  element(){return document.getElementById('viewportDebugText');},
  set(message){
    const el=this.element();
    if(el)el.textContent=String(message??'');
    return String(message??'');
  },
  renderer(){
    let state='unknown';
    try{
      const snap=RendererRuntimeState?.snapshot?.();
      state=snap?.degraded?'degraded':(snap?.rendererReady?'ready':'not-ready');
    }catch(_){}
    const job=RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;
    const diag=LiteTraceDiagnostics.snapshot(job);
    const perf=diag.performance?.performance||RenderCoreH?.performance?.(job)||null;
    const workers=diag.performance?.workers||RenderFramework?.jobs?.workerStats?.()||null;
    const tile=job?RenderCoreH?.tileSize?.(job):null;
    const engine=job?.settings?.engine||'—';
    const rays=perf?.totalRays||0;
    const rps=perf?.raysPerSecond||job?.progress?.raysPerSecond||0;
    const samples=perf?.samples||job?.progress?.currentSamples||0;
    const renderMs=Math.round(perf?.stageMs?.render||0);
    return this.set(
      `LiteTrace ${LiteTrace?.version||'1.0 RC2'} | 3DLite v${ThreeDLiteVersion.version} | ${engine}`+
      ` | renderer=${state}`+
      ` | workers=${workers?.workers??0}/${workers?.configuredSize??0}`+
      ` | tile=${tile??'—'}`+
      ` | rays=${rays}`+
      ` | rps=${rps}`+
      ` | samples=${samples}`+
      ` | render=${renderMs}ms`+
      ` | viewport=${document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady||'false'}`+
      ` | error=${document.documentElement.dataset.rendererLastError||'none'}`
    );
  },
  error(error,stage='runtime'){
    const msg=String(error?.message||error||'unknown error').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    return this.set(`3DLite DEBUG | stage=${stage} | ${msg}`);
  },
  async copy(){
    const text=this.element()?.textContent||'';
    try{
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    }catch(_){
      const ta=document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value=text;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';
      document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();
      try{document.execCommand('copy');}catch(__){}
      ta.remove();
    }
    return text;
  }
});
window.ViewportDebugLine=ViewportDebugLine;
window.set3DLiteDebugLine=(message)=>ViewportDebugLine.set(message);
window.copy3DLiteDebugLine=()=>ViewportDebugLine.copy();

document.getElementById('viewportDebugCopy')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  const b=document.getElementById('viewportDebugCopy');
  await ViewportDebugLine.copy();
  if(b){const old=b.textContent;b.textContent='Copied';setTimeout(()=>b.textContent=old,700);}
});

var ViewportDebugLine=Object.freeze({
  element(){return document.getElementById('viewportDebugText');},
  set(message){
    const el=this.element();
    if(el)el.textContent=String(message??'');
    return String(message??'');
  },
  renderer(){
    let state='unknown';
    try{
      const snap=RendererRuntimeState?.snapshot?.();
      state=snap?.degraded?'degraded':(snap?.rendererReady?'ready':'not-ready');
    }catch(_){}
    const job=RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;
    const diag=LiteTraceDiagnostics.snapshot(job);
    const perf=diag.performance?.performance||RenderCoreH?.performance?.(job)||null;
    const workers=diag.performance?.workers||RenderFramework?.jobs?.workerStats?.()||null;
    const tile=job?RenderCoreH?.tileSize?.(job):null;
    const engine=job?.settings?.engine||'—';
    const rays=perf?.totalRays||0;
    const rps=perf?.raysPerSecond||job?.progress?.raysPerSecond||0;
    const samples=perf?.samples||job?.progress?.currentSamples||0;
    const renderMs=Math.round(perf?.stageMs?.render||0);
    return this.set(
      `LiteTrace ${LiteTrace?.version||'1.0 RC2'} | 3DLite v${ThreeDLiteVersion.version} | ${engine}`+
      ` | renderer=${state}`+
      ` | workers=${workers?.workers??0}/${workers?.configuredSize??0}`+
      ` | tile=${tile??'—'}`+
      ` | rays=${rays}`+
      ` | rps=${rps}`+
      ` | samples=${samples}`+
      ` | render=${renderMs}ms`+
      ` | viewport=${document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady||'false'}`+
      ` | error=${document.documentElement.dataset.rendererLastError||'none'}`
    );
  },
  error(error,stage='runtime'){
    const msg=String(error?.message||error||'unknown error').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    return this.set(`3DLite DEBUG | stage=${stage} | ${msg}`);
  },
  async copy(){
    const text=this.element()?.textContent||'';
    try{
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    }catch(_){
      const ta=document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value=text;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';
      document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();
      try{document.execCommand('copy');}catch(__){}
      ta.remove();
    }
    return text;
  }
});

function boot3DLite(){
  ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.EDITOR_BOOTING,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.EDITOR);
  register3DLiteCoreServices();
  const serviceDeps=validate3DLiteServiceDependencies();
  if(!serviceDeps.ok)console.warn('[3DLite] service dependency warnings:',serviceDeps.missing);
  if(__3DLiteBooted||window.__3DLiteBooting)return;

  const required=[
    ['THREE',window.THREE],
    ['OrbitControls',window.THREE?.OrbitControls],
    ['TransformControls',window.THREE?.TransformControls]
  ];
  const missing=required.filter(([,v])=>!v).map(([n])=>n);
  if(missing.length){
    window.__3DLiteBooted=false;
    window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Missing required viewport dependency: '+missing.join(', '));
    return;
  }

  window.__3DLiteBooting=true;
  document.documentElement.dataset.editorState='booting';
  document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady='false';
  document.documentElement.dataset.rendererReady='false';

  EditorContext=new EditorContextClass();
  window.EditorContext=EditorContext;
  const arch=validateArchitectureCore();
  if(!arch.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Architecture validation failed: '+arch.errors.join(', '));return;}
  const archTests=runArchitectureSelfTests();
  if(!archTests.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Architecture self-te

eBooted=false;
    window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Missing required viewport dependency: '+missing.join(', '));

  document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady='false';
  document.documentElement.dataset.rendererReady='false';

  EditorContext=new EditorContextClass();
  window.EditorContext=EditorContext;
  const arch=validateArchitectureCore();
  if(!arch.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Architecture validation failed: '+arch.errors.join(', '));return;}
  const archTests=runArchitectureSelfTests();
  if(!archTests.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Architecture self-test failed: '+archTests.failures.join(', '));return;}
  const transformValidation=validateTransformSystem();
  if(!transformValidation.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Transform validation failed: '+transformValidation.errors.join(', '));return;}
  const transformTests=runTransformSystemSelfTests();
  if(!transformTests.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Transform self-test failed: '+transformTests.failures.join(', '));return;}
  document.documentElement.dataset.architectureSelfTest='passed';

  requestRender();
  requestStatsUpdate();
  document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady='true';
  document.documentElement.dataset.editorState='ready';
  ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.EDITOR_READY,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.VIEWPORT);
  window.__3DLiteBootRenderReady=true;

  // LiteTrace starts after the modeling viewport is ready; renderer failure is fail-soft.
  queueMicrotask(()=>{
    ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.RENDERER_BOOTING,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.RENDERER);
    try{
      const rendererBoot=RendererBootstrap.initialize();
      if(!rendererBoot.ok)RendererBootstrap.showFailure(new RendererError('bootstrap',rendererBoot.errors.join('; ')));
      const rendererRelease=runRendererReleaseGate();
      window.__3DLiteRendererReleaseCandidate=rendererRelease.ok;
      window.__3DLiteRendererReleaseWarnings=rendererRelease.ok?[]:[...(rendererRelease.failures||[])];
      if(!rendererRelease.ok)console.warn('[LiteTrace] Non-blocking release-gate warnings:',rendererRelease.failures);
      ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(
        rendererBoot.ok?ThreeDLiteLifecycle.READY:ThreeDLiteLifecycle.DEGRADED,
        ThreeDLiteBootPhase.READY
      );
      ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();
    }catch(err){
      ThreeDLiteAppContext.fail('renderer-bootstrap',err);
      ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.DEGRADED,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.READY);
      console.error('[LiteTrace] deferred initialization failed',err);
      try{ViewportDebugLine?.error?.(err,'renderer-bootstrap');}catch(_){}
    }
  });
  if(!window.__3DLiteAnimationLoopStarted){window.__3DLiteAnimationLoopStarted=true;animate(performance.now());}

  __3DLiteBooted=true;
  window.__3DLiteBooted=true;
  window.__3DLiteBooting=false;
  document.documentElement.dataset.editorBooted='true';
  ViewportDebugLine.renderer();
  document.documentElement.dataset.architectureCore='3.78.28-gen3-transform-system';
  document.documentElement.dataset.transformSystem='3.78.28-gen3';
  document.documentElement.dataset.blenderMaxIntegrated='true';
}

/* ---------- v3.78.19 MeshWireManager ----------
   Persistent LineSegments2 wire objects backed by LineSegmentsGeometry.
   Topology and geometry edits update the existing wire object safely.
*/
var MeshWireManager={
  ensure(mesh){
    let wire=mesh?.userData?.wire;
    if(wire && wire.userData?.persistentWire && wire.geometry?.isLineSegmentsGeometry)return wire;

    if(wire){
      mesh.remove(wire);
      wire.geometry?.dispose?.();
      wire.material?.dispose?.();
    }

    const material=cleanLineMaterial(COLORS.wire,1.0,.98);
    const geometry=new THREE.LineSegmentsGeometry();
    geometry.setPositions(new Float32Array(0));
    wire=new THREE.LineSegments2(geometry,material);
    wire.name='__wire';
    wire.raycast=()=>{};
    wire.renderOrder=2;
    wire.frustumCulled=false;
    wire.userData.persistentWire=true;
    mesh.add(wire);
    mesh.userData.wire=wire;
    return wire;
  },

    const src=positions instanceof Float32Array ? positions : new Float32Array(positions||[]);

/* ---------- v3.78.19 MeshWireManager ----------
   Persistent LineSegments2 wire objects backed by LineSegmentsGeometry.
   Topology and geometry edits update the existing wire object safely.
*/
var MeshWireManager={
  ensure(mesh){
    let wire=mesh?.userData?.wire;
    if(wire && wire.userData?.persistentWire && wire.geometry?.isLineSegmentsGeometry)return wire;

    if(wire){
      mesh.remove(wire);
      wire.geometry?.dispose?.();
      wire.material?.dispose?.();
    }

    const material=cleanLineMaterial(COLORS.wire,1.0,.98);
    const geometry=new THREE.LineSegmentsGeometry();
    geometry.setPositions(new Float32Array(0));
    wire=new THREE.LineSegments2(geometry,material);
    wire.name='__wire';
    wire.raycast=()=>{};
    wire.renderOrder=2;
    wire.frustumCulled=false;
    wire.userData.persistentWire=true;
    mesh.add(wire);
    mesh.userData.wire=wire;
    return wire;
  },

  writePositions(wire,positions){
    if(!wire)return;
    const src=positions instanceof Float32Array ? positions : new Float32Array(positions||[]);
    wire.geometry.setPositions(src);
    wire.computeLineDistances?.();
    wire.geometry.computeBoundingBox?.();
    wire.geometry.computeBoundingSphere?.();
  },

  editablePositions(mesh){
    const ep=mesh?.userData?.editPoly;
    if(!ep)return new Float32Array(0);
    const edges=epEdges(ep);
    const out=new Float32Array(edges.length*6);
    let k=0;
    for(const e of edges){
      const a=ep.v[e.a]?.p,b=ep.v[e.b]?.p;
      if(!a||!b)continue;
      out[k++]=a.x;out[k++]=a.y;out[k++]=a.z;
      out[k++]=b.x;out[k++]=b.y;out[k++]=b.z;
    }
    return k===out.length?out:out.slice(0,k);
  },

  topologyPositions(mesh){
    if(mesh?.userData?.editPoly)return this.editablePositions(mesh);

    let g=null;
    if(mesh?.userData?.primitiveType==='box')g=boxTopologyWireGeometry(mesh);
    else g=polygonBoundaryW

/* ---------- v3.78.19 MeshWireManager ----------
   Persistent LineSegments2 wire objects backed by LineSegmentsGeometry.
   Topology and geometry edits update the existing wire object safely.
*/
var MeshWireManager={
  ensure(mesh){
    let wire=mesh?.userData?.wire;
    if(wire && wire.userData?.persistentWire && wire.geometry?.isLineSegmentsGeometry)return wire;

    if(wire){
      mesh.remove(wire);
      wire.geometry?.dispose?.();
      wire.material?.dispose?.();
    }

    const material=cleanLineMaterial(COLORS.wire,1.0,.98);
    const geometry=new THREE.LineSegmentsGeometry();
    geometry.setPositions(new Float32Array(0));
    wire=new THREE.LineSegments2(geometry,material);
    wire.name='__wire';
    wire.raycast=()=>{};
    wire.renderOrder=2;
    wire.frustumCulled=false;
    wire.userData.persistentWire=true;
    mesh.add(wire);
    mesh.userData.wire=wire;
    return wire;
  },

  writePositions(wire,positions){
    if(!wire)return;
    const src=positions instanceof Float32Array ? positions : new Float32Array(positions||[]);
    wire.geometry.setPositions(src);
    wire.computeLineDistances?.();
    wire.geometry.computeBoundingBox?.();
    wire.geometry.computeBoundingSphere?.();
  },

  editablePositions(mesh){
    const ep=mesh?.userData?.editPoly;
    if(!ep)return new Float32Array(0);
    const edges=epEdges(ep);
    const out=new Float32Array(edges.length*6);
    let k=0;
    for(const e of edges){
      const a=ep.v[e.a]?.p,b=ep.v[e.b]?.p;
      if(!a||!b)continue;
      out[k++]=a.x;out[k++]=a.y;out[k++]=a.z;
      out[k++]=b.x;out[k++]=b.y;out[k++]=b.z;
    }
    return k===out.length?out:out.slice(0,k);
  },

  topologyPositions(mesh){
    if(mesh?.userData?.editPoly)return this.editablePositions(mesh);

    let g=null;
    if(mesh?.userData?.primitiveType==='box')g=boxTopologyWireGeometry(mesh);
    else g=polygonBoundaryWireGeometry(mesh.geometry);

    const attr=g?.getAttribute?.('position');
    const arr=attr?new Float32Array(attr.array):new Float32Array(0);
    g?.dispose?.();
    return arr;
  },

  rebuild(mesh){
    if(!mesh)return;
    const wire=this.ensure(mesh);
    this.writePositions(wire,this.topologyPositions(mesh));
    const ep=mesh.userData?.editPoly;DerivedMeshCache.state(mesh).wireRevision=ep?.topologyVersion??0;
    updateDisplayForMesh(mesh);
  },

  function learn(report){
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NDebugRegistry?.register?.({
      category:CODES[report.code]?.category||'rendering',subtype:report.code.toLowerCase(),severity:report.severity,
      subsystem:'l3n-rendering-debugger',source:'render-debugger',message:report.code+' '+report.name+(report.message?': '+report.message:''),
      reproduced:true,regression:report.severity==='critical'||report.severity==='high',evidence:report,status:report.status||'open'
    });}catch(_){}
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NTargetLearning?.record?.(15,'learning','render debugger '+report.code+' '+report.name);}catch(_){}
  }

  function record(code='RND-0001',input={}){
    if(!CODES[code])code='RND-0001';
    const meta=CODES[code];
    const report={schema:1,version:VERSION,code,name:meta.name,severity:String(input.severity||meta.severity),category:meta.category,
      detectedAt:now(),renderId:input.renderId??input.evidence?.renderId??state.active?.renderId??null,
      stage:String(input.stage||input.evidence?.errorStage||'render'),message:String(input.message||''),status:String(input.status||'open'),
      evidence:safe(input.evidence||{}),recommendedAction:String(input.recommendedAction||''),lastSuccessfulStage:String(input.lastSuccessfulStage||'')};
    state.reports.push(report);if(state.reports.length>MAX_REPORTS)state.reports.shift();state.last=report;state.lastCode=code;learn(report);renderPanel();return Object.freeze({...report});
  }
